1) % len(WAVE_PALETTES)]\n    return palette[kind] if palette else None","originalFile":"import math\nimport random\nimport pygame\n\nWIDTH, HEIGHT = 600, 700\nPLAYER_Y, PLAYER_SPEED, SHIP_GAP = HEIGHT - 50, 300, 30\nENTRY_TIME = 2.0\n\n\ndef bezier(p0, p1, p2, p3, t):\n    u = 1 - t\n    x = u ** 3 * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[0] + t ** 3 * p3[0]\n    y = u ** 3 * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[1] + t ** 3 * p3[1]\n    return pygame.Vector2(x, y)\n\n\ndef enemy_tint(kind):\n    \"\"\"Return an (r, g, b) colour override for an enemy kind, or None for the default.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\ndef on_wave_start(wave):\n    \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\ndef shield_charges(wave):\n    \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\nENEMY_COLORS = {\"boss\": (90, 220, 90), \"red\": (230, 70, 70), \"blue\": (80, 140, 240)}\n\n\nclass Enemy:\n    def __init__(self, kind, home, index, from_left):\n        self.kind, self.home = kind, pygame.Vector2(home)\n        self.hp = 2 if kind == \"boss\" else 1\n        self.state, self.t, self.delay = \"entry\", 0.0, index * 0.12\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(-40 if from_left else WIDTH + 40, 120)\n        side = 1 if from_left else -1\n        self.path = ((self.pos.x, 120), (self.pos.x + side * 250, 420), (WIDTH / 2 - side * 200, 60), (home[0], home[1]))\n        self.duration = ENTRY_TIME\n\n    def start_dive(self, player_x):\n        side = random.choice([-1, 1])\n        end = (random.randint(60, WIDTH - 60), HEIGHT + 40)\n        self.path = (tuple(self.pos), (self.pos.x + side * 160, self.pos.y + 160), (player_x, HEIGHT - 200), end)\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"dive\", 0.0, 2.6\n\n    def start_return(self):\n        top = (self.home.x, -30)\n        self.path = (top, (top[0] + 80, 120), (self.home.x - 80, 200), tuple(self.home))\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"entry\", 0.0, 1.5\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(top)\n\n    def update(self, dt, sway, player_x, shots):\n        if self.delay > 0:\n            self.delay -= dt\n            return\n        if self.state == \"formation\":\n            self.pos = self.home + pygame.Vector2(sway, 0)\n            return\n        self.t += dt / self.duration\n        if self.state == \"dive\" and random.random() < dt * 0.8 and self.pos.y < HEIGHT - 250:\n            shots.append(pygame.Vector2(self.pos))\n        if self.t >= 1:\n            if self.state == \"dive\":\n                self.start_return()\n            else:\n                self.state = \"formation\"\n            return\n        self.pos = bezier(*self.path, self.t)\n\n\ndef spawn_wave(wave):\n    enemies, index = [], 0\n    layout = [(\"boss\", 4, 90), (\"red\", 8, 130), (\"red\", 8, 170), (\"blue\", 8, 210)]\n    for kind, count, y in layout:\n        for i in range(count):\n            x = WIDTH / 2 + (i - (count - 1) / 2) * 52\n            enemies.append(Enemy(kind, (x, y), index, index % 2 == 0))\n            index += 1\n    on_wave_start(wave)\n    return enemies\n\n\nclass Game:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.stars = [[random.randint(0, WIDTH), random.randint(0, HEIGHT), random.uniform(30, 120)] for _ in range(70)]\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self):\n        self.x, self.ships, self.lives = WIDTH / 2, 1, 3\n        self.score, self.wave, self.time = 0, 1, 0.0\n        self.bullets, self.shots = [], []\n        self.cooldown, self.invulnerable, self.dive_timer = 0.0, 0.0, 3.0\n        self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n        self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n        self.state = \"play\"\n\n    def ship_xs(self):\n        return [self.x + i * SHIP_GAP for i in range(self.ships)]\n\n    def fire(self):\n        if self.cooldown <= 0 and len(self.bullets) < 4 * self.ships:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                self.bullets.append(pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y - 16))\n            self.cooldown = 0.25\n\n    def hit_player(self):\n        if self.invulnerable > 0:\n            return\n        if self.shield > 0:\n            self.shield -= 1\n            self.invulnerable = 1.0\n            return\n        if self.ships > 1:\n            self.ships -= 1\n        else:\n            self.lives -= 1\n            if self.lives <= 0:\n                self.state = \"lose\"\n        self.invulnerable = 2.0\n\n    def kill(self, enemy):\n        self.enemies.remove(enemy)\n        self.score += 100 if enemy.state == \"dive\" else 50\n        if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.state == \"dive\":\n            self.ships = 2\n\n    def update(self, dt, keys):\n        if self.state != \"play\":\n            return\n        self.time += dt\n        self.cooldown -= dt\n        self.invulnerable = max(0.0, self.invulnerable - dt)\n        span = SHIP_GAP * (self.ships - 1)\n        move = keys[pygame.K_RIGHT] - keys[pygame.K_LEFT]\n        self.x = max(20, min(WIDTH - 20 - span, self.x + move * PLAYER_SPEED * dt))\n        if keys[pygame.K_SPACE]:\n            self.fire()\n        for star in self.stars:\n            star[1] = (star[1] + star[2] * dt) % HEIGHT\n        sway = math.sin(self.time * 1.3) * 24\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            enemy.update(dt, sway, self.x, self.shots)\n        self.dive_timer -= dt\n        ready = [e for e in self.enemies if e.state == \"formation\"]\n        if self.dive_timer <= 0 and ready:\n            random.choice(ready).start_dive(self.x)\n            self.dive_timer = max(0.8, 3.0 - self.wave * 0.3)\n        self.update_projectiles(dt)\n        if not self.enemies:\n            self.wave += 1\n            self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n            self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n\n    def update_projectiles(self, dt):\n        for bullet in self.bullets[:]:\n            bullet.y -= 520 * dt\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.delay <= 0 and enemy.pos.distance_to(bullet) < 18:\n                    enemy.hp -= 1\n                    if enemy.hp <= 0:\n                        self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.bullets.remove(bullet)\n                    break\n        self.bullets = [b for b in self.bullets if b.y > -10]\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            shot.y += 260 * dt\n        self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.y < HEIGHT]\n        for sx in self.ship_xs():\n            target = pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y)\n            if any(s.distance_to(target) < 14 for s in self.shots):\n                self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.distance_to(target) >= 14]\n                self.hit_player()\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.state == \"dive\" and enemy.pos.distance_to(target) < 22:\n                    self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.hit_player()\n                    break\n\n    def draw(self, screen, font):\n        screen.fill((5, 5, 20))\n        for x, y, speed in self.stars:\n            shade = int(80 + speed)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (shade, shade, shade), (int(x), int(y)), 1)\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            if enemy.delay > 0:\n                continue\n            color = enemy_tint(enemy.kind) or ENEMY_COLORS[enemy.kind]\n            if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.hp == 1:\n                color = (170, 90, 220)\n            x, y = enemy.pos\n            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, color, [(x - 14, y - 8), (x + 14, y - 8), (x + 8, y + 10), (x - 8, y + 10)])\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x - 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x + 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n        for bullet in self.bullets:\n            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 255, 120), (bullet.x - 2, bullet.y - 8, 4, 14))\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 120, 60), (int(shot.x), int(shot.y)), 4)\n        if self.invulnerable <= 0 or int(self.invulnerable * 10) % 2 == 0:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (230, 230, 240), [(sx, PLAYER_Y - 18), (sx + 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (220, 60, 60), [(sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 6, PLAYER_Y - 2), (sx - 4, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n        hud = font.render(f\"Score {self.score}  Lives {self.lives}  Wave {self.wave}  R = reset\", True, (240, 240, 240))\n        screen.blit(hud, (10, 8))\n        if self.state == \"lose\":\n            label = font.render(\"GAME OVER - Press R\", True, (255, 255, 120))\n            screen.blit(label, label.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)))\n\n\ndef main():\n    pygame.init()\n    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))\n    pygame.display.set_caption(\"Galaga\")\n    clock = pygame.time.Clock()\n    font = pygame.font.Font(None, 26)\n    game = Game()\n    running = True\n    while running:\n        dt = min(clock.tick(60) / 1000, 0.05)\n        for event in pygame.event.get():\n            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:\n                running = False\n            elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_r:\n                game.reset()\n        game.update(dt, pygame.key.get_pressed())\n        game.draw(screen, font)\n        pygame.display.flip()\n    pygame.quit()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":14,"oldLines":9,"newStart":14,"newLines":19,"lines":["     return pygame.Vector2(x, y)"," "," ","+tint_wave = 1","+# Palettes cycle by wave; None keeps ENEMY_COLORS. (file state is current in your context â€” no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"9f4705ec-5d0f-47a3-84a5-b37658982fc7","timestamp":"2026-10-08T14:05:22.640Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga\\game.py","oldString":"def spawn_wave(wave):\n    enemies, index = [], 0","newString":"def spawn_wave(wave):\n    global tint_wave\n    tint_wave = wave\n    enemies, index = [], 0","originalFile":"import math\nimport random\nimport pygame\n\nWIDTH, HEIGHT = 600, 700\nPLAYER_Y, PLAYER_SPEED, SHIP_GAP = HEIGHT - 50, 300, 30\nENTRY_TIME = 2.0\n\n\ndef bezier(p0, p1, p2, p3, t):\n    u = 1 - t\n    x = u ** 3 * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[0] + t ** 3 * p3[0]\n    y = u ** 3 * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[1] + t ** 3 * p3[1]\n    return pygame.Vector2(x, y)\n\n\ntint_wave = 1\n# Palettes cycle by wave; None keeps ENEMY_COLORS. Hues avoid the damaged-boss purple.\nWAVE_PALETTES = [\n    None,\n    {\"boss\": (240, 200, 60), \"red\": (240, 110, 170), \"blue\": (60, 200, 200)},\n    {\"boss\": (230, 140, 50), \"red\": (200, 50, 100), \"blue\": (120, 230, 140)},\n]\n\n\ndef enemy_tint(kind):\n    \"\"\"Return an (r, g, b) colour override for an enemy kind, or None for the default.\"\"\"\n    palette = WAVE_PALETTES[(tint_wave - 1) % len(WAVE_PALETTES)]\n    return palette[kind] if palette else None\n\n\ndef on_wave_start(wave):\n    \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\ndef shield_charges(wave):\n    \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\nENEMY_COLORS = {\"boss\": (90, 220, 90), \"red\": (230, 70, 70), \"blue\": (80, 140, 240)}\n\n\nclass Enemy:\n    def __init__(self, kind, home, index, from_left):\n        self.kind, self.home = kind, pygame.Vector2(home)\n        self.hp = 2 if kind == \"boss\" else 1\n        self.state, self.t, self.delay = \"entry\", 0.0, index * 0.12\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(-40 if from_left else WIDTH + 40, 120)\n        side = 1 if from_left else -1\n        self.path = ((self.pos.x, 120), (self.pos.x + side * 250, 420), (WIDTH / 2 - side * 200, 60), (home[0], home[1]))\n        self.duration = ENTRY_TIME\n\n    def start_dive(self, player_x):\n        side = random.choice([-1, 1])\n        end = (random.randint(60, WIDTH - 60), HEIGHT + 40)\n        self.path = (tuple(self.pos), (self.pos.x + side * 160, self.pos.y + 160), (player_x, HEIGHT - 200), end)\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"dive\", 0.0, 2.6\n\n    def start_return(self):\n        top = (self.home.x, -30)\n        self.path = (top, (top[0] + 80, 120), (self.home.x - 80, 200), tuple(self.home))\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"entry\", 0.0, 1.5\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(top)\n\n    def update(self, dt, sway, player_x, shots):\n        if self.delay > 0:\n            self.delay -= dt\n            return\n        if self.state == \"formation\":\n            self.pos = self.home + pygame.Vector2(sway, 0)\n            return\n        self.t += dt / self.duration\n        if self.state == \"dive\" and random.random() < dt * 0.8 and self.pos.y < HEIGHT - 250:\n            shots.append(pygame.Vector2(self.pos))\n        if self.t >= 1:\n            if self.state == \"dive\":\n                self.start_return()\n            else:\n                self.state = \"formation\"\n            return\n        self.pos = bezier(*self.path, self.t)\n\n\ndef spawn_wave(wave):\n    enemies, index = [], 0\n    layout = [(\"boss\", 4, 90), (\"red\", 8, 130), (\"red\", 8, 170), (\"blue\", 8, 210)]\n    for kind, count, y in layout:\n        for i in range(count):\n            x = WIDTH / 2 + (i - (count - 1) / 2) * 52\n            enemies.append(Enemy(kind, (x, y), index, index % 2 == 0))\n            index += 1\n    on_wave_start(wave)\n    return enemies\n\n\nclass Game:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.stars = [[random.randint(0, WIDTH), random.randint(0, HEIGHT), random.uniform(30, 120)] for _ in range(70)]\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self):\n        self.x, self.ships, self.lives = WIDTH / 2, 1, 3\n        self.score, self.wave, self.time = 0, 1, 0.0\n        self.bullets, self.shots = [], []\n        self.cooldown, self.invulnerable, self.dive_timer = 0.0, 0.0, 3.0\n        self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n        self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n        self.state = \"play\"\n\n    def ship_xs(self):\n        return [self.x + i * SHIP_GAP for i in range(self.ships)]\n\n    def fire(self):\n        if self.cooldown <= 0 and len(self.bullets) < 4 * self.ships:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                self.bullets.append(pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y - 16))\n            self.cooldown = 0.25\n\n    def hit_player(self):\n        if self.invulnerable > 0:\n            return\n        if self.shield > 0:\n            self.shield -= 1\n            self.invulnerable = 1.0\n            return\n        if self.ships > 1:\n            self.ships -= 1\n        else:\n            self.lives -= 1\n            if self.lives <= 0:\n                self.state = \"lose\"\n        self.invulnerable = 2.0\n\n    def kill(self, enemy):\n        self.enemies.remove(enemy)\n        self.score += 100 if enemy.state == \"dive\" else 50\n        if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.state == \"dive\":\n            self.ships = 2\n\n    def update(self, dt, keys):\n        if self.state != \"play\":\n            return\n        self.time += dt\n        self.cooldown -= dt\n        self.invulnerable = max(0.0, self.invulnerable - dt)\n        span = SHIP_GAP * (self.ships - 1)\n        move = keys[pygame.K_RIGHT] - keys[pygame.K_LEFT]\n        self.x = max(20, min(WIDTH - 20 - span, self.x + move * PLAYER_SPEED * dt))\n        if keys[pygame.K_SPACE]:\n            self.fire()\n        for star in self.stars:\n            star[1] = (star[1] + star[2] * dt) % HEIGHT\n        sway = math.sin(self.time * 1.3) * 24\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            enemy.update(dt, sway, self.x, self.shots)\n        self.dive_timer -= dt\n        ready = [e for e in self.enemies if e.state == \"formation\"]\n        if self.dive_timer <= 0 and ready:\n            random.choice(ready).start_dive(self.x)\n            self.dive_timer = max(0.8, 3.0 - self.wave * 0.3)\n        self.update_projectiles(dt)\n        if not self.enemies:\n            self.wave += 1\n            self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n            self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n\n    def update_projectiles(self, dt):\n        for bullet in self.bullets[:]:\n            bullet.y -= 520 * dt\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.delay <= 0 and enemy.pos.distance_to(bullet) < 18:\n                    enemy.hp -= 1\n                    if enemy.hp <= 0:\n                        self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.bullets.remove(bullet)\n                    break\n        self.bullets = [b for b in self.bullets if b.y > -10]\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            shot.y += 260 * dt\n        self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.y < HEIGHT]\n        for sx in self.ship_xs():\n            target = pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y)\n            if any(s.distance_to(target) < 14 for s in self.shots):\n                self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.distance_to(target) >= 14]\n                self.hit_player()\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.state == \"dive\" and enemy.pos.distance_to(target) < 22:\n                    self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.hit_player()\n                    break\n\n    def draw(self, screen, font):\n        screen.fill((5, 5, 20))\n        for x, y, speed in self.stars:\n            shade = int(80 + speed)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (shade, shade, shade), (int(x), int(y)), 1)\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            if enemy.delay > 0:\n                continue\n            color = enemy_tint(enemy.kind) or ENEMY_COLORS[enemy.kind]\n            if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.hp == 1:\n                color = (170, 90, 220)\n            x, y = enemy.pos\n            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, color, [(x - 14, y - 8), (x + 14, y - 8), (x + 8, y + 10), (x - 8, y + 10)])\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x - 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x + 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n        for bullet in self.bullets:\n            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 255, 120), (bullet.x - 2, bullet.y - 8, 4, 14))\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 120, 60), (int(shot.x), int(shot.y)), 4)\n        if self.invulnerable <= 0 or int(self.invulnerable * 10) % 2 == 0:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (230, 230, 240), [(sx, PLAYER_Y - 18), (sx + 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (220, 60, 60), [(sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 6, PLAYER_Y - 2), (sx - 4, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n        hud = font.render(f\"Score {self.score}  Lives {self.lives}  Wave {self.wave}  R = reset\", True, (240, 240, 240))\n        screen.blit(hud, (10, 8))\n        if self.state == \"lose\":\n            label = font.render(\"GAME OVER - Press R\", True, (255, 255, 120))\n            screen.blit(label, label.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)))\n\n\ndef main():\n    pygame.init()\n    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))\n    pygame.display.set_caption(\"Galaga\")\n    clock = pygame.time.Clock()\n    font = pygame.font.Font(None, 26)\n    game = Game()\n    running = True\n    while running:\n        dt = min(clock.tick(60) / 1000, 0.05)\n        for event in pygame.event.get():\n            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:\n                running = False\n            elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_r:\n                game.reset()\n        game.update(dt, pygame.key.get_pressed())\n        game.draw(screen, font)\n        pygame.display.flip()\n    pygame.quit()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":84,"oldLines":6,"newStart":84,"newLines":8,"lines":[" "," "," def spawn_wave(wave):","+    global tint_wave","+    tint_wave = wave","     enemies, index = [], 0","     layout = [(\"boss\", 4, 90), (\"red\", 8, 130), (\"red\", 8, 170), (\"blue\", 8, 210)]","     for kind, count, y in layout:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2cf6833b-d314-4804-bc38-0498d5f46cbc","session_id":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga","sessionId":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}

g = game.Game(); keys = [0]*512\nfor f in range(60*60):\n    keys[pygame.K_SPACE] = 1; keys[pygame.K_LEFT] = (f // 90) % 2; keys[pygame.K_RIGHT] = 1 - keys[pygame.K_LEFT]\n    g.update(1/60, pygame.key.ScancodeWrapper(keys)); g.draw(screen, font)\nprint(f\"60 s autoplay: wave={g.wave} score={g.score} lives={g.lives} state={g.state} tint_wave={game.tint_wave}\")\ng.reset(); print(\"after reset: tint_wave =\", game.tint_wave, \"tint(red) =\", game.enemy_tint(\"red\"))\nEOF\necho \"--- real game run ---\"; (file state is current in your context â€” no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"6f46b66d-cdba-4029-a86d-8db1113a2f03","timestamp":"2026-10-08T14:10:41.934Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga\\game.py","oldString":"def on_wave_start(wave):\n    \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"\n    pass","newString":"banner_text, banner_until = \"\", 0\nBANNER_MS = 1000\n\n\ndef on_wave_start(wave):\n    \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"\n    global banner_text, banner_until\n    banner_text, banner_until = f\"WAVE {wave}\", pygame.time.get_ticks() + BANNER_MS","originalFile":"import math\nimport random\nimport pygame\n\nWIDTH, HEIGHT = 600, 700\nPLAYER_Y, PLAYER_SPEED, SHIP_GAP = HEIGHT - 50, 300, 30\nENTRY_TIME = 2.0\n\n\ndef bezier(p0, p1, p2, p3, t):\n    u = 1 - t\n    x = u ** 3 * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[0] + t ** 3 * p3[0]\n    y = u ** 3 * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[1] + t ** 3 * p3[1]\n    return pygame.Vector2(x, y)\n\n\ntint_wave = 1\n# Palettes cycle by wave; None keeps ENEMY_COLORS. Hues avoid the damaged-boss purple.\nWAVE_PALETTES = [\n    None,\n    {\"boss\": (240, 200, 60), \"red\": (240, 110, 170), \"blue\": (60, 200, 200)},\n    {\"boss\": (230, 140, 50), \"red\": (200, 50, 100), \"blue\": (120, 230, 140)},\n]\n\n\ndef enemy_tint(kind):\n    \"\"\"Return an (r, g, b) colour override for an enemy kind, or None for the default.\"\"\"\n    palette = WAVE_PALETTES[(tint_wave - 1) % len(WAVE_PALETTES)]\n    return palette[kind] if palette else None\n\n\ndef on_wave_start(wave):\n    \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\ndef shield_charges(wave):\n    \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\nENEMY_COLORS = {\"boss\": (90, 220, 90), \"red\": (230, 70, 70), \"blue\": (80, 140, 240)}\n\n\nclass Enemy:\n    def __init__(self, kind, home, index, from_left):\n        self.kind, self.home = kind, pygame.Vector2(home)\n        self.hp = 2 if kind == \"boss\" else 1\n        self.state, self.t, self.delay = \"entry\", 0.0, index * 0.12\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(-40 if from_left else WIDTH + 40, 120)\n        side = 1 if from_left else -1\n        self.path = ((self.pos.x, 120), (self.pos.x + side * 250, 420), (WIDTH / 2 - side * 200, 60), (home[0], home[1]))\n        self.duration = ENTRY_TIME\n\n    def start_dive(self, player_x):\n        side = random.choice([-1, 1])\n        end = (random.randint(60, WIDTH - 60), HEIGHT + 40)\n        self.path = (tuple(self.pos), (self.pos.x + side * 160, self.pos.y + 160), (player_x, HEIGHT - 200), end)\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"dive\", 0.0, 2.6\n\n    def start_return(self):\n        top = (self.home.x, -30)\n        self.path = (top, (top[0] + 80, 120), (self.home.x - 80, 200), tuple(self.home))\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"entry\", 0.0, 1.5\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(top)\n\n    def update(self, dt, sway, player_x, shots):\n        if self.delay > 0:\n            self.delay -= dt\n            return\n        if self.state == \"formation\":\n            self.pos = self.home + pygame.Vector2(sway, 0)\n            return\n        self.t += dt / self.duration\n        if self.state == \"dive\" and random.random() < dt * 0.8 and self.pos.y < HEIGHT - 250:\n            shots.append(pygame.Vector2(self.pos))\n        if self.t >= 1:\n            if self.state == \"dive\":\n                self.start_return()\n            else:\n                self.state = \"formation\"\n            return\n        self.pos = bezier(*self.path, self.t)\n\n\ndef spawn_wave(wave):\n    global tint_wave\n    tint_wave = wave\n    enemies, index = [], 0\n    layout = [(\"boss\", 4, 90), (\"red\", 8, 130), (\"red\", 8, 170), (\"blue\", 8, 210)]\n    for kind, count, y in layout:\n        for i in range(count):\n            x = WIDTH / 2 + (i - (count - 1) / 2) * 52\n            enemies.append(Enemy(kind, (x, y), index, index % 2 == 0))\n            index += 1\n    on_wave_start(wave)\n    return enemies\n\n\nclass Game:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.stars = [[random.randint(0, WIDTH), random.randint(0, HEIGHT), random.uniform(30, 120)] for _ in range(70)]\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self):\n        self.x, self.ships, self.lives = WIDTH / 2, 1, 3\n        self.score, self.wave, self.time = 0, 1, 0.0\n        self.bullets, self.shots = [], []\n        self.cooldown, self.invulnerable, self.dive_timer = 0.0, 0.0, 3.0\n        self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n        self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n        self.state = \"play\"\n\n    def ship_xs(self):\n        return [self.x + i * SHIP_GAP for i in range(self.ships)]\n\n    def fire(self):\n        if self.cooldown <= 0 and len(self.bullets) < 4 * self.ships:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                self.bullets.append(pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y - 16))\n            self.cooldown = 0.25\n\n    def hit_player(self):\n        if self.invulnerable > 0:\n            return\n        if self.shield > 0:\n            self.shield -= 1\n            self.invulnerable = 1.0\n            return\n        if self.ships > 1:\n            self.ships -= 1\n        else:\n            self.lives -= 1\n            if self.lives <= 0:\n                self.state = \"lose\"\n        self.invulnerable = 2.0\n\n    def kill(self, enemy):\n        self.enemies.remove(enemy)\n        self.score += 100 if enemy.state == \"dive\" else 50\n        if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.state == \"dive\":\n            self.ships = 2\n\n    def update(self, dt, keys):\n        if self.state != \"play\":\n            return\n        self.time += dt\n        self.cooldown -= dt\n        self.invulnerable = max(0.0, self.invulnerable - dt)\n        span = SHIP_GAP * (self.ships - 1)\n        move = keys[pygame.K_RIGHT] - keys[pygame.K_LEFT]\n        self.x = max(20, min(WIDTH - 20 - span, self.x + move * PLAYER_SPEED * dt))\n        if keys[pygame.K_SPACE]:\n            self.fire()\n        for star in self.stars:\n            star[1] = (star[1] + star[2] * dt) % HEIGHT\n        sway = math.sin(self.time * 1.3) * 24\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            enemy.update(dt, sway, self.x, self.shots)\n        self.dive_timer -= dt\n        ready = [e for e in self.enemies if e.state == \"formation\"]\n        if self.dive_timer <= 0 and ready:\n            random.choice(ready).start_dive(self.x)\n            self.dive_timer = max(0.8, 3.0 - self.wave * 0.3)\n        self.update_projectiles(dt)\n        if not self.enemies:\n            self.wave += 1\n            self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n            self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n\n    def update_projectiles(self, dt):\n        for bullet in self.bullets[:]:\n            bullet.y -= 520 * dt\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.delay <= 0 and enemy.pos.distance_to(bullet) < 18:\n                    enemy.hp -= 1\n                    if enemy.hp <= 0:\n                        self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.bullets.remove(bullet)\n                    break\n        self.bullets = [b for b in self.bullets if b.y > -10]\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            shot.y += 260 * dt\n        self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.y < HEIGHT]\n        for sx in self.ship_xs():\n            target = pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y)\n            if any(s.distance_to(target) < 14 for s in self.shots):\n                self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.distance_to(target) >= 14]\n                self.hit_player()\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.state == \"dive\" and enemy.pos.distance_to(target) < 22:\n                    self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.hit_player()\n                    break\n\n    def draw(self, screen, font):\n        screen.fill((5, 5, 20))\n        for x, y, speed in self.stars:\n            shade = int(80 + speed)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (shade, shade, shade), (int(x), int(y)), 1)\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            if enemy.delay > 0:\n                continue\n            color = enemy_tint(enemy.kind) or ENEMY_COLORS[enemy.kind]\n            if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.hp == 1:\n                color = (170, 90, 220)\n            x, y = enemy.pos\n            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, color, [(x - 14, y - 8), (x + 14, y - 8), (x + 8, y + 10), (x - 8, y + 10)])\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x - 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x + 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n        for bullet in self.bullets:\n            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 255, 120), (bullet.x - 2, bullet.y - 8, 4, 14))\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 120, 60), (int(shot.x), int(shot.y)), 4)\n        if self.invulnerable <= 0 or int(self.invulnerable * 10) % 2 == 0:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (230, 230, 240), [(sx, PLAYER_Y - 18), (sx + 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (220, 60, 60), [(sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 6, PLAYER_Y - 2), (sx - 4, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n        hud = font.render(f\"Score {self.score}  Lives {self.lives}  Wave {self.wave}  R = reset\", True, (240, 240, 240))\n        screen.blit(hud, (10, 8))\n        if self.state == \"lose\":\n            label = font.render(\"GAME OVER - Press R\", True, (255, 255, 120))\n            screen.blit(label, label.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)))\n\n\ndef main():\n    pygame.init()\n    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))\n    pygame.display.set_caption(\"Galaga\")\n    clock = pygame.time.Clock()\n    font = pygame.font.Font(None, 26)\n    game = Game()\n    running = True\n    while running:\n        dt = min(clock.tick(60) / 1000, 0.05)\n        for event in pygame.event.get():\n            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:\n                running = False\n            elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_r:\n                game.reset()\n        game.update(dt, pygame.key.get_pressed())\n        game.draw(screen, font)\n        pygame.display.flip()\n    pygame.quit()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":29,"oldLines":9,"newStart":29,"newLines":14,"lines":["     return palette[kind] if palette else None"," "," ","+banner_text, banner_until = \"\", 0","+BANNER_MS = 1000","+","+"," def on_wave_start(wave):","     \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"","-    pass","+    global banner_text, banner_until","+    banner_text, banner_until = f\"WAVE {wave}\", pygame.time.get_ticks() + BANNER_MS"," "," "," def shield_charges(wave):"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a2d23528-ab84-48ba-bfc5-bea494f00226","session_id":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga","sessionId":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"bcfdb0e5-7cb3-49dc-bcc8-53e2bc802344","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"4e273cea-adbf-46a2-af5c-b87c7a4bc5de","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EKm8ZdHQPeD9jPZfjqKWpz","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga\\game.py has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context â€” no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"645c7244-da48-4339-96c6-1fca88689c30","timestamp":"2026-10-08T14:10:42.038Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga\\game.py","oldString":"        screen.blit(hud, (10, 8))\n        if self.state == \"lose\":","newString":"        screen.blit(hud, (10, 8))\n        if pygame.time.get_ticks() < banner_until:\n            banner = font.render(banner_text, True, (255, 255, 120))\n            screen.blit(banner, banner.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)))\n        if self.state == \"lose\":","originalFile":"import math\nimport random\nimport pygame\n\nWIDTH, HEIGHT = 600, 700\nPLAYER_Y, PLAYER_SPEED, SHIP_GAP = HEIGHT - 50, 300, 30\nENTRY_TIME = 2.0\n\n\ndef bezier(p0, p1, p2, p3, t):\n    u = 1 - t\n    x = u ** 3 * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[0] + t ** 3 * p3[0]\n    y = u ** 3 * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[1] + t ** 3 * p3[1]\n    return pygame.Vector2(x, y)\n\n\ntint_wave = 1\n# Palettes cycle by wave; None keeps ENEMY_COLORS. Hues avoid the damaged-boss purple.\nWAVE_PALETTES = [\n    None,\n    {\"boss\": (240, 200, 60), \"red\": (240, 110, 170), \"blue\": (60, 200, 200)},\n    {\"boss\": (230, 140, 50), \"red\": (200, 50, 100), \"blue\": (120, 230, 140)},\n]\n\n\ndef enemy_tint(kind):\n    \"\"\"Return an (r, g, b) colour override for an enemy kind, or None for the default.\"\"\"\n    palette = WAVE_PALETTES[(tint_wave - 1) % len(WAVE_PALETTES)]\n    return palette[kind] if palette else None\n\n\nbanner_text, banner_until = \"\", 0\nBANNER_MS = 1000\n\n\ndef on_wave_start(wave):\n    \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"\n    global banner_text, banner_until\n    banner_text, banner_until = f\"WAVE {wave}\", pygame.time.get_ticks() + BANNER_MS\n\n\ndef shield_charges(wave):\n    \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\nENEMY_COLORS = {\"boss\": (90, 220, 90), \"red\": (230, 70, 70), \"blue\": (80, 140, 240)}\n\n\nclass Enemy:\n    def __init__(self, kind, home, index, from_left):\n        self.kind, self.home = kind, pygame.Vector2(home)\n        self.hp = 2 if kind == \"boss\" else 1\n        self.state, self.t, self.delay = \"entry\", 0.0, index * 0.12\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(-40 if from_left else WIDTH + 40, 120)\n        side = 1 if from_left else -1\n        self.path = ((self.pos.x, 120), (self.pos.x + side * 250, 420), (WIDTH / 2 - side * 200, 60), (home[0], home[1]))\n        self.duration = ENTRY_TIME\n\n    def start_dive(self, player_x):\n        side = random.choice([-1, 1])\n        end = (random.randint(60, WIDTH - 60), HEIGHT + 40)\n        self.path = (tuple(self.pos), (self.pos.x + side * 160, self.pos.y + 160), (player_x, HEIGHT - 200), end)\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"dive\", 0.0, 2.6\n\n    def start_return(self):\n        top = (self.home.x, -30)\n        self.path = (top, (top[0] + 80, 120), (self.home.x - 80, 200), tuple(self.home))\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"entry\", 0.0, 1.5\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(top)\n\n    def update(self, dt, sway, player_x, shots):\n        if self.delay > 0:\n            self.delay -= dt\n            return\n        if self.state == \"formation\":\n            self.pos = self.home + pygame.Vector2(sway, 0)\n            return\n        self.t += dt / self.duration\n        if self.state == \"dive\" and random.random() < dt * 0.8 and self.pos.y < HEIGHT - 250:\n            shots.append(pygame.Vector2(self.pos))\n        if self.t >= 1:\n            if self.state == \"dive\":\n                self.start_return()\n            else:\n                self.state = \"formation\"\n            return\n        self.pos = bezier(*self.path, self.t)\n\n\ndef spawn_wave(wave):\n    global tint_wave\n    tint_wave = wave\n    enemies, index = [], 0\n    layout = [(\"boss\", 4, 90), (\"red\", 8, 130), (\"red\", 8, 170), (\"blue\", 8, 210)]\n    for kind, count, y in layout:\n        for i in range(count):\n            x = WIDTH / 2 + (i - (count - 1) / 2) * 52\n            enemies.append(Enemy(kind, (x, y), index, index % 2 == 0))\n            index += 1\n    on_wave_start(wave)\n    return enemies\n\n\nclass Game:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.stars = [[random.randint(0, WIDTH), random.randint(0, HEIGHT), random.uniform(30, 120)] for _ in range(70)]\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self):\n        self.x, self.ships, self.lives = WIDTH / 2, 1, 3\n        self.score, self.wave, self.time = 0, 1, 0.0\n        self.bullets, self.shots = [], []\n        self.cooldown, self.invulnerable, self.dive_timer = 0.0, 0.0, 3.0\n        self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n        self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n        self.state = \"play\"\n\n    def ship_xs(self):\n        return [self.x + i * SHIP_GAP for i in range(self.ships)]\n\n    def fire(self):\n        if self.cooldown <= 0 and len(self.bullets) < 4 * self.ships:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                self.bullets.append(pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y - 16))\n            self.cooldown = 0.25\n\n    def hit_player(self):\n        if self.invulnerable > 0:\n            return\n        if self.shield > 0:\n            self.shield -= 1\n            self.invulnerable = 1.0\n            return\n        if self.ships > 1:\n            self.ships -= 1\n        else:\n            self.lives -= 1\n            if self.lives <= 0:\n                self.state = \"lose\"\n        self.invulnerable = 2.0\n\n    def kill(self, enemy):\n        self.enemies.remove(enemy)\n        self.score += 100 if enemy.state == \"dive\" else 50\n        if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.state == \"dive\":\n            self.ships = 2\n\n    def update(self, dt, keys):\n        if self.state != \"play\":\n            return\n        self.time += dt\n        self.cooldown -= dt\n        self.invulnerable = max(0.0, self.invulnerable - dt)\n        span = SHIP_GAP * (self.ships - 1)\n        move = keys[pygame.K_RIGHT] - keys[pygame.K_LEFT]\n        self.x = max(20, min(WIDTH - 20 - span, self.x + move * PLAYER_SPEED * dt))\n        if keys[pygame.K_SPACE]:\n            self.fire()\n        for star in self.stars:\n            star[1] = (star[1] + star[2] * dt) % HEIGHT\n        sway = math.sin(self.time * 1.3) * 24\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            enemy.update(dt, sway, self.x, self.shots)\n        self.dive_timer -= dt\n        ready = [e for e in self.enemies if e.state == \"formation\"]\n        if self.dive_timer <= 0 and ready:\n            random.choice(ready).start_dive(self.x)\n            self.dive_timer = max(0.8, 3.0 - self.wave * 0.3)\n        self.update_projectiles(dt)\n        if not self.enemies:\n            self.wave += 1\n            self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n            self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n\n    def update_projectiles(self, dt):\n        for bullet in self.bullets[:]:\n            bullet.y -= 520 * dt\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.delay <= 0 and enemy.pos.distance_to(bullet) < 18:\n                    enemy.hp -= 1\n                    if enemy.hp <= 0:\n                        self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.bullets.remove(bullet)\n                    break\n        self.bullets = [b for b in self.bullets if b.y > -10]\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            shot.y += 260 * dt\n        self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.y < HEIGHT]\n        for sx in self.ship_xs():\n            target = pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y)\n            if any(s.distance_to(target) < 14 for s in self.shots):\n                self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.distance_to(target) >= 14]\n                self.hit_player()\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.state == \"dive\" and enemy.pos.distance_to(target) < 22:\n                    self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.hit_player()\n                    break\n\n    def draw(self, screen, font):\n        screen.fill((5, 5, 20))\n        for x, y, speed in self.stars:\n            shade = int(80 + speed)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (shade, shade, shade), (int(x), int(y)), 1)\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            if enemy.delay > 0:\n                continue\n            color = enemy_tint(enemy.kind) or ENEMY_COLORS[enemy.kind]\n            if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.hp == 1:\n                color = (170, 90, 220)\n            x, y = enemy.pos\n            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, color, [(x - 14, y - 8), (x + 14, y - 8), (x + 8, y + 10), (x - 8, y + 10)])\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x - 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x + 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n        for bullet in self.bullets:\n            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 255, 120), (bullet.x - 2, bullet.y - 8, 4, 14))\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 120, 60), (int(shot.x), int(shot.y)), 4)\n        if self.invulnerable <= 0 or int(self.invulnerable * 10) % 2 == 0:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (230, 230, 240), [(sx, PLAYER_Y - 18), (sx + 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (220, 60, 60), [(sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 6, PLAYER_Y - 2), (sx - 4, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n        hud = font.render(f\"Score {self.score}  Lives {self.lives}  Wave {self.wave}  R = reset\", True, (240, 240, 240))\n        screen.blit(hud, (10, 8))\n        if self.state == \"lose\":\n            label = font.render(\"GAME OVER - Press R\", True, (255, 255, 120))\n            screen.blit(label, label.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)))\n\n\ndef main():\n    pygame.init()\n    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))\n    pygame.display.set_caption(\"Galaga\")\n    clock = pygame.time.Clock()\n    font = pygame.font.Font(None, 26)\n    game = Game()\n    running = True\n    while running:\n        dt = min(clock.tick(60) / 1000, 0.05)\n        for event in pygame.event.get():\n            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:\n                running = False\n            elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_r:\n                game.reset()\n        game.update(dt, pygame.key.get_pressed())\n        game.draw(screen, font)\n        pygame.display.flip()\n    pygame.quit()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":223,"oldLines":6,"newStart":223,"newLines":9,"lines":["                 pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (220, 60, 60), [(sx - g = game.Game()\nrun(g, \"wave 1 (game start)\", shot=f\"{sys.argv[1]}/banner_wave1.png\")\ng.enemies = []; run(g, \"wave 2 (after clear)\")\ng.enemies = []; run(g, \"wave 3 (after clear)\")\ng.reset(); run(g, \"R reset -> wave 1\")\nprint(\"tint still works:\", game.enemy_tint(\"red\"), \"(wave 1 default)\", \"| wave\", g.wave)\nEOF\necho \"--- real game run ---\"; SDL_VIDEODRIVER=dummy SDL_AUDIODRIVER=dummy timeout 6 python game.py >/dev/null; {"parentUuid":"deea887f-75b2-43c6-ad2e-fdc91f1f3a4a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfpuUMyfoc1kv1mbC5Rfe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Task 3 works: a \"WAVE X\" banner appears in the centre of the screen at the start of every wave and disappears after about one second. The game keeps running while it shows. Only `game.py` changed, and nothing is committed.\n\n**What I changed in `game.py`**\n\n1. **Two new module variables and a constant, above `on_wave_start`:** `banner_text`, `banner_until` and `BANNER_MS = 1000`. `on_wave_start` only receives the wave number, and its return value is ignored, so these are how it tells `draw()` what to show and until when.\n2. **`on_wave_start(wave)`:** replaces `pass`. It sets the text to `\"WAVE {wave}\"` and the expiry to the current time plus 1000 ms, using `pygame.time.get_ticks()`.\n3. **`Game.draw()`:** three lines added after the score line. While the expiry time hasn't passed, it draws the banner centred on the screen with the existing font and the same yellow as \"GAME OVER\".\n\nNo `time.sleep()` and no change to `update()` or the main loop; the banner disappears once the time passes. I didn't change `spawn_wave` (it already calls the hook at the right place), the tint code, controls, movement, shooting, collision, scoring, lives or `shield_charges`.\n\n**The diff:** it contains only the Task 3 changes above. Task 2 was already committed (`b6180d2`), so it doesn't appear.\n\n**Testing (real time, at 60 fps)**\n\n| Case | Banner text | Visible for | Then gone |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Game start | WAVE 1 | ~994 ms | yes |\n| After wave 1 cleared | WAVE 2 | ~999 ms | yes |\n| After wave 2 cleared | WAVE 3 | ~1003 ms | yes |\n| `R` reset | WAVE 1 | ~989 ms | yes |\n\n- **Game keeps running:** while the banner was up, game time advanced and enemies flew in. About 9 had started moving by the end of the second, which matches the 0.12 s gap between enemy entries.\n- **Screenshot:** the wave-1 frame shows the banner centred while the first enemies fly in. The wave-1 default colours still apply, so Task 2 is unaffected.\n- **Real game:** `python game.py` ran for 6 seconds with no display and didn't crash.\n\nTwo things to know:\n- The banner uses the same font size as the score line. You asked for a simple version, so I didn't add a larger font. It's easy to add if you want it bigger.\n- The banner times itself with real time, not game time. That would only matter if the game ever paused, which it currently can't."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1604,"cache_read_input_tokens":75629,"output_tokens":883,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1604,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":883,"cache_read_input_tokens":75629,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1604,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1604},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"db20c6a6-2e9f-4cf3-84af-f840487bb0dc","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfpuUMUfiLTiwyj3XxcUV","type":"assistant","uuid":"9750d06d-9082-47f3-81b2-1c63db194442","timestamp":"2026-10-08T14:11:35.151Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga","sessionId":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context â€” no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"468fa73e-8e75-45b5-b19b-54887bdf7e38","timestamp":"2026-10-08T14:13:41.292Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga\\game.py","oldString":"    \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n    pass","newString":"    \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n    return 1 if wave % 3 == 0 else 0","originalFile":"import math\nimport random\nimport pygame\n\nWIDTH, HEIGHT = 600, 700\nPLAYER_Y, PLAYER_SPEED, SHIP_GAP = HEIGHT - 50, 300, 30\nENTRY_TIME = 2.0\n\n\ndef bezier(p0, p1, p2, p3, t):\n    u = 1 - t\n    x = u ** 3 * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[0] + t ** 3 * p3[0]\n    y = u ** 3 * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[1] + t ** 3 * p3[1]\n    return pygame.Vector2(x, y)\n\n\ntint_wave = 1\n# Palettes cycle by wave; None keeps ENEMY_COLORS. Hues avoid the damaged-boss purple.\nWAVE_PALETTES = [\n    None,\n    {\"boss\": (240, 200, 60), \"red\": (240, 110, 170), \"blue\": (60, 200, 200)},\n    {\"boss\": (230, 140, 50), \"red\": (200, 50, 100), \"blue\": (120, 230, 140)},\n]\n\n\ndef enemy_tint(kind):\n    \"\"\"Return an (r, g, b) colour override for an enemy kind, or None for the default.\"\"\"\n    palette = WAVE_PALETTES[(tint_wave - 1) % len(WAVE_PALETTES)]\n    return palette[kind] if palette else None\n\n\nbanner_text, banner_until = \"\", 0\nBANNER_MS = 1000\n\n\ndef on_wave_start(wave):\n    \"\"\"Called at the start of every wave; add banners, speed-ups, or palette swaps here.\"\"\"\n    global banner_text, banner_until\n    banner_text, banner_until = f\"WAVE {wave}\", pygame.time.get_ticks() + BANNER_MS\n\n\ndef shield_charges(wave):\n    \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\nENEMY_COLORS = {\"boss\": (90, 220, 90), \"red\": (230, 70, 70), \"blue\": (80, 140, 240)}\n\n\nclass Enemy:\n    def __init__(self, kind, home, index, from_left):\n        self.kind, self.home = kind, pygame.Vector2(home)\n        self.hp = 2 if kind == \"boss\" else 1\n        self.state, self.t, self.delay = \"entry\", 0.0, index * 0.12\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(-40 if from_left else WIDTH + 40, 120)\n        side = 1 if from_left else -1\n        self.path = ((self.pos.x, 120), (self.pos.x + side * 250, 420), (WIDTH / 2 - side * 200, 60), (home[0], home[1]))\n        self.duration = ENTRY_TIME\n\n    def start_dive(self, player_x):\n        side = random.choice([-1, 1])\n        end = (random.randint(60, WIDTH - 60), HEIGHT + 40)\n        self.path = (tuple(self.pos), (self.pos.x + side * 160, self.pos.y + 160), (player_x, HEIGHT - 200), end)\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"dive\", 0.0, 2.6\n\n    def start_return(self):\n        top = (self.home.x, -30)\n        self.path = (top, (top[0] + 80, 120), (self.home.x - 80, 200), tuple(self.home))\n        self.state, self.t, self.duration = \"entry\", 0.0, 1.5\n        self.pos = pygame.Vector2(top)\n\n    def update(self, dt, sway, player_x, shots):\n        if self.delay > 0:\n            self.delay -= dt\n            return\n        if self.state == \"formation\":\n            self.pos = self.home + pygame.Vector2(sway, 0)\n            return\n        self.t += dt / self.duration\n        if self.state == \"dive\" and random.random() < dt * 0.8 and self.pos.y < HEIGHT - 250:\n            shots.append(pygame.Vector2(self.pos))\n        if self.t >= 1:\n            if self.state == \"dive\":\n                self.start_return()\n            else:\n                self.state = \"formation\"\n            return\n        self.pos = bezier(*self.path, self.t)\n\n\ndef spawn_wave(wave):\n    global tint_wave\n    tint_wave = wave\n    enemies, index = [], 0\n    layout = [(\"boss\", 4, 90), (\"red\", 8, 130), (\"red\", 8, 170), (\"blue\", 8, 210)]\n    for kind, count, y in layout:\n        for i in range(count):\n            x = WIDTH / 2 + (i - (count - 1) / 2) * 52\n            enemies.append(Enemy(kind, (x, y), index, index % 2 == 0))\n            index += 1\n    on_wave_start(wave)\n    return enemies\n\n\nclass Game:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.stars = [[random.randint(0, WIDTH), random.randint(0, HEIGHT), random.uniform(30, 120)] for _ in range(70)]\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self):\n        self.x, self.ships, self.lives = WIDTH / 2, 1, 3\n        self.score, self.wave, self.time = 0, 1, 0.0\n        self.bullets, self.shots = [], []\n        self.cooldown, self.invulnerable, self.dive_timer = 0.0, 0.0, 3.0\n        self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n        self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n        self.state = \"play\"\n\n    def ship_xs(self):\n        return [self.x + i * SHIP_GAP for i in range(self.ships)]\n\n    def fire(self):\n        if self.cooldown <= 0 and len(self.bullets) < 4 * self.ships:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                self.bullets.append(pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y - 16))\n            self.cooldown = 0.25\n\n    def hit_player(self):\n        if self.invulnerable > 0:\n            return\n        if self.shield > 0:\n            self.shield -= 1\n            self.invulnerable = 1.0\n            return\n        if self.ships > 1:\n            self.ships -= 1\n        else:\n            self.lives -= 1\n            if self.lives <= 0:\n                self.state = \"lose\"\n        self.invulnerable = 2.0\n\n    def kill(self, enemy):\n        self.enemies.remove(enemy)\n        self.score += 100 if enemy.state == \"dive\" else 50\n        if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.state == \"dive\":\n            self.ships = 2\n\n    def update(self, dt, keys):\n        if self.state != \"play\":\n            return\n        self.time += dt\n        self.cooldown -= dt\n        self.invulnerable = max(0.0, self.invulnerable - dt)\n        span = SHIP_GAP * (self.ships - 1)\n        move = keys[pygame.K_RIGHT] - keys[pygame.K_LEFT]\n        self.x = max(20, min(WIDTH - 20 - span, self.x + move * PLAYER_SPEED * dt))\n        if keys[pygame.K_SPACE]:\n            self.fire()\n        for star in self.stars:\n            star[1] = (star[1] + star[2] * dt) % HEIGHT\n        sway = math.sin(self.time * 1.3) * 24\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            enemy.update(dt, sway, self.x, self.shots)\n        self.dive_timer -= dt\n        ready = [e for e in self.enemies if e.state == \"formation\"]\n        if self.dive_timer <= 0 and ready:\n            random.choice(ready).start_dive(self.x)\n            self.dive_timer = max(0.8, 3.0 - self.wave * 0.3)\n        self.update_projectiles(dt)\n        if not self.enemies:\n            self.wave += 1\n            self.enemies = spawn_wave(self.wave)\n            self.shield = shield_charges(self.wave) or 0\n\n    def update_projectiles(self, dt):\n        for bullet in self.bullets[:]:\n            bullet.y -= 520 * dt\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.delay <= 0 and enemy.pos.distance_to(bullet) < 18:\n                    enemy.hp -= 1\n                    if enemy.hp <= 0:\n                        self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.bullets.remove(bullet)\n                    break\n        self.bullets = [b for b in self.bullets if b.y > -10]\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            shot.y += 260 * dt\n        self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.y < HEIGHT]\n        for sx in self.ship_xs():\n            target = pygame.Vector2(sx, PLAYER_Y)\n            if any(s.distance_to(target) < 14 for s in self.shots):\n                self.shots = [s for s in self.shots if s.distance_to(target) >= 14]\n                self.hit_player()\n            for enemy in self.enemies:\n                if enemy.state == \"dive\" and enemy.pos.distance_to(target) < 22:\n                    self.kill(enemy)\n                    self.hit_player()\n                    break\n\n    def draw(self, screen, font):\n        screen.fill((5, 5, 20))\n        for x, y, speed in self.stars:\n            shade = int(80 + speed)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (shade, shade, shade), (int(x), int(y)), 1)\n        for enemy in self.enemies:\n            if enemy.delay > 0:\n                continue\n            color = enemy_tint(enemy.kind) or ENEMY_COLORS[enemy.kind]\n            if enemy.kind == \"boss\" and enemy.hp == 1:\n                color = (170, 90, 220)\n            x, y = enemy.pos\n            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, color, [(x - 14, y - 8), (x + 14, y - 8), (x + 8, y + 10), (x - 8, y + 10)])\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x - 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 255, 255), (int(x + 5), int(y - 2)), 2)\n        for bullet in self.bullets:\n            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 255, 120), (bullet.x - 2, bullet.y - 8, 4, 14))\n        for shot in self.shots:\n            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 120, 60), (int(shot.x), int(shot.y)), 4)\n        if self.invulnerable <= 0 or int(self.invulnerable * 10) % 2 == 0:\n            for sx in self.ship_xs():\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (230, 230, 240), [(sx, PLAYER_Y - 18), (sx + 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n                pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (220, 60, 60), [(sx - 14, PLAYER_Y + 12), (sx - 6, PLAYER_Y - 2), (sx - 4, PLAYER_Y + 12)])\n        hud = font.render(f\"Score {self.score}  Lives {self.lives}  Wave {self.wave}  R = reset\", True, (240, 240, 240))\n        screen.blit(hud, (10, 8))\n        if pygame.time.get_ticks() < banner_until:\n            banner = font.render(banner_text, True, (255, 255, 120))\n            screen.blit(banner, banner.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)))\n        if self.state == \"lose\":\n            label = font.render(\"GAME OVER - Press R\", True, (255, 255, 120))\n            screen.blit(label, label.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)))\n\n\ndef main():\n    pygame.init()\n    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))\n    pygame.display.set_caption(\"Galaga\")\n    clock = pygame.time.Clock()\n    font = pygame.font.Font(None, 26)\n    game = Game()\n    running = True\n    while running:\n        dt = min(clock.tick(60) / 1000, 0.05)\n        for event in pygame.event.get():\n            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:\n                running = False\n            elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_r:\n                game.reset()\n        game.update(dt, pygame.key.get_pressed())\n        game.draw(screen, font)\n        pygame.display.flip()\n    pygame.quit()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":41,"oldLines":7,"newStart":41,"newLines":7,"lines":[" "," def shield_charges(wave):","     \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"","-    pass","+    return 1 if wave % 3 == 0 else 0"," "," "," ENEMY_COLORS = {\"boss\": (90, 220, 90), \"red\": (230, 70, 70), \"blue\": (80, 140, 240)}"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a04c3113-a084-4ade-b0f0-30649ce246ac","session_id":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Downloads\\SE lab 4\\31_galaga","sessionId":"80c3398a-5751-42c9-a42d-db96f6ff1101","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}

It's a one-line change in `game.py`, and nothing is committed.\n\n**The change**\n```diff\n def shield_charges(wave):\n     \"\"\"Return how many hits the player's shield can absorb this wave, or None to disable the shield.\"\"\"\n-    pass\n+    return 1 if wave % 3 == 0 else 0\n```\nThis is the only change in the diff. Tasks 1â€“3 are already committed (latest `56a2e1f`) and untouched. I didn't change `hit_player()` or anything else.\n\n**One correction to the brief:** `shield_charges()` isn't called from `spawn_wave()`. It's called from `Game.reset()` (`game.py:116`) and `Game.update()` (`game.py:174`), each right after `spawn_wave()`. The timing is the same, so it works as the README intends. Because the value is reassigned each wave, an unused charge doesn't carry over to the next wave.\n\n**Testing**\n- **Charges per wave:**\n\n  | Wave | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 | 7 | 8 | 9 |\n  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n  | Shield charges | 0 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 1 |\n\n  The game's own shield value matched this table for each wave when I cleared waves 1â†’9 through the normal game code.\n- **Charges are used up correctly on wave 3:**\n  - The first hit uses the charge, keeps the ship and life, and gives 1 s of invulnerability.\n  - A hit during that second does nothing.\n  - The next hit costs a life as normal (3 â†’ 2).\n  - A real enemy shot hitting the ship during normal play also used the charge instead of a life.\n- **No shield on wave 1:** a hit costs a life as before.\n- **No carry-over:** an unused wave-3 charge resets to 0 on wave 4.\n- **Reset:** `R` brings back wave 1 with no shield, the default colours and the \"WAVE 1\" banner, so Tasks 2 and 3 still work.\n- **Real game:** `python game.py` ran for 6 seconds with no display and didn't crash.\n\nThe screen doesn't show how many shield charges you have, so in your \"after\" video the shield will only show up as a hit that doesn't cost a ship, followed by a short blink.
